# Explainability record

Question: For each department, how many available courses could fulfill a particular  elective group in a selected term? Instance: advanced elective group; Fall 2026.

Mode: grounded

## Generated text (LLM)

For each department, the number of available courses that could fulfill the advanced elective group in Fall 2026 is as follows:

- CS: 50 courses [R1]
- ECE: 46 courses [R2]
- MATH: 30 courses [R3]
- STAT: 19 courses [R4]
- PHYS: 17 courses [R5]
- CSE: 12 courses [R6]
- ASRM: 5 courses [R7]
- PHIL: 3 courses [R8]
- BIOE: 2 courses [R9]
- MSE: 2 courses [R10]
- ADV: 1 course [R11]
- AE: 1 course [R12]
- IE: 1 course [R13]
- ME: 1 course [R14]
- NE: 1 course [R15]
- TAM: 1 course [R16]

This data is derived directly from the provided rows in the context, where each row specifies a department and the count of courses offered in the advanced elective group during Fall 2026. The counts are explicitly stated in the "advancedElectivesOfferedFall2026" field for each department. No additional reasoning or inference is required to establish this count, as the values are directly provided in the query results.

Note: The graph evidence (edges) confirms that courses are scheduled in Fall 2026 for various departments, and the "hasCourse" and "scheduledInTerm" relationships support the existence of these courses. However, the count per department in the advanced elective group is not derived from a set of course titles or attributes but is instead a direct value in the row data. Therefore, the provided numbers represent the total number of courses offered by each department in the advanced elective group during Fall 2026, as reported in the dataset.

- [E223e1a9ab76696bf] `<https://example.org/uiuc-course-advisor/resource/requirement/UIUC-BS-CS-2026/Elective-Technical> <https://example.org/uiuc-course-advisor/ontology#requirementNote> "At least 6 additional technical electives totalling at least 18 credit hours, chosen from CS 397 and CS 400-level courses, not including CS 400, CS 401, CS 402, CS 403 or CS 491. One \"CS-like\" course in another department may count as a CS 400-level course with CS Academic Office permission; non-CS technical electives are not considered in focus areas. Allowed courses are generated from catalog data by rule R4."`
